# Instagram engagement log

Started 2026-09-10. Session logged in as nolanpigi via browser (2FA completed once, "Trust this device" checked).
Rules: business accounts only, never personal profiles. Likes at human pacing (60-120s between actions).
Caps (Nolan's 2026-09-15 heavy directive; weekly ramp +5 likes/+3 comments per 2026-09-26 no-scale-back order): up to 55 likes + 15 comments per pass, effective pass 133 (2026-09-26). 50 likes + 12 comments before the ramp. One browser task per pass on this account; parallelism across platforms only.
Comments: under 20 words, funny first, then punchy, expert/techy but down to earth, post-specific. Never sell, never mention PING Social, no links, no contact info, no customer-posing. Never use em dashes.
Format preference (Nolan's call 2026-09-10): prefer regular public feed posts (photos, carousels) over reels; only engage a reel if it visibly has public comments already.
Stop instantly on any action block, checkpoint, or warning and report it.
NEVER like or comment on the same account twice across passes. The off-limits list is PER-ACCOUNT: never engage ANY post on an already-engaged handle, even an older post never engaged before. (Pass 18 violation: the browser task re-engaged 5 pass-12 accounts on older posts because the old "same account/post" phrasing was read as per-post; every pass brief must now state the per-account block in unmistakable terms.)
MECHANICAL ENFORCEMENT (2026-09-14): prose is not the enforcement. Every candidate must pass ~/workspace/outreach/engagement-guard.py (filter against this log) before action, completed actions are recorded back into this log immediately via the guard, and the pass is verified against the pre-pass snapshot afterwards. An LLM that forgets the rule still cannot engage a blocked account.

## 2026-09-19 — Pass 72 (completed ~09:35 MDT)
CLASSIFICATION: SUCCESS_EMPTY (genuine empty, enumeration verified healthy). 0 likes, 0 comments.

RECOVERY CONTEXT: the 32-pass Pig CDP wedge streak ended this morning. At ~07:13 MDT ig-recovery-test.sh (exclusive writer) verified the full chain (CDP ok, login gate ok, enumerate ok with 30 entries, dry-run ok, one canary like executed and verified) and flipped ig-capability-state.json to AVAILABLE. The manual close/reopen of the ig-profile Chrome window was the required operator action; it was observed. canary_required carried a file/ledger note mismatch (state file false, truth note true for first scheduled run); the verified canary from the recovery test itself satisfies the requirement, and this pass ran read-only plus zero actions. This pass is the first scheduled run on the recovered capability.

Phase A ran 3 discovery strategies via run_ig_discover.sh (read-only, signed in as nolanpigi; login gate passed every run). All 3 ran healthy: 14 queries each, zero anomalies, CDP + session stable.
- Strategy 1 (trade+city typeahead: "gj plumber", "durango hvac", ...): 15 near-misses, 0 candidates. Typeahead returned junk JG-prefixed non-Western-CO accounts and stale/no-post profiles.
- Strategy 2 (city-first + hashtag: "grand junction colorado", "#durangoplumber", ...): 20 near-misses, 0 candidates. Surfaced city/tourism/food/photography accounts, no trades.
- Strategy 3 (small-town trade: "cortez plumber", "bayfield electrician", "pagosa hvac", ...): 14 near-misses, 0 candidates. Found real Western CO trade handles (@meekerelectric, @craigroofer, @aspen_hvac, @aspenair_hvac) but all have no posts or no extractable post link.
